* commit 1d57b3b1e812a8f9c9c2c32347ec5b4f1e1cd73c
| Author: Bảo Ngọc Hà <hiembaonon@gmail.com>
| Date:   Thu Aug 6 00:20:36 2026 +0700
| 
|     test(fr-02): add validated case data for the login and lockout suite
|     
|     14 cases mapping all 15 HW02 manual cases plus 2 security cases designed in
|     HW04. Data-only per §6 — the spec dispatches on an assertion vocabulary rather
|     than on case IDs.
|     
|     Recon against the running build changed three design decisions, recorded in
|     test-design/FR-02-login/case-map.md:
|     - the lock lands after 2 consecutive failures, not the 3 the spec requires, so
|       every case that submits a wrong password registers its own throwaway account
|     - BUG-FR02-004 is not re-encoded as an assertion: the oracle demands the error
|       message NOT disclose the cause, so asserting a lock-specific message would
|       contradict the requirement it claims to test
|     - only the email half of BUG-FR02-002 has an oracle; the password-field
|       observation goes to not-automated.md as a finding without a requirement

* commit 7e78dc6492621f2cc29c01a9949ecec7e147fdd8
| Author: Bảo Ngọc Hà <hiembaonon@gmail.com>
| Date:   Thu Aug 6 00:13:57 2026 +0700
| 
|     test(matrix): merge manifest rows across runs and stamp ISO timestamps in ICT
|     
|     A filtered run (--feature/--browser) rewrote the whole manifest, dropping the
|     rows of every cell it did not execute. Rows are now keyed by (feature, browser)
|     and only the executed cells are replaced, so the on-camera FR-02 re-run keeps
|     the other six cells' results.
|     
|     Timestamps switch from toISOString()'s UTC to ISO 8601 with the +07:00 offset:
|     a run before 07:00 ICT was stamped with the previous day's date and no longer
|     matched its own commit date.

* commit 6c49ae8df5f9f8f828e44ca6e288dec535486ccc
| Author: Bảo Ngọc Hà <hiembaonon@gmail.com>
| Date:   Wed Aug 5 23:37:21 2026 +0700
| 
|     test(automation): scaffold data-driven Playwright project for the 3x3 matrix
|     
|     TypeScript + @playwright/test project that the three feature specs will plug
|     into. No feature cases yet — smoke.spec.ts only checks the SUT is reachable
|     before a matrix run.
|     
|     - playwright.config.ts: chromium/firefox/webkit projects, workers=1 and
|       fullyParallel=false (EShop is one SQLite file), retries=0 so genuine defects
|       stay visible, 90s timeout for FR-02's real 30s lockout wait.
|     - utils/config.ts: .env-driven identity/URLs, validated at startup. STUDENT_ID
|       never appears in a spec.
|     - utils/data.ts: the only way into test-data/*.json — schema validation,
|       duplicate-ID detection, and the >=12-records-per-feature gate from SS6.
|     - utils/api.ts: setup/teardown client (throwaway users, fresh orders, live
|       dashboard totals). Feature behaviour is still driven through the UI.
|     - utils/stamp.ts: writes "Run by: <id>" + ISO timestamp into each report as a
|       visible banner and <title>. Playwright 1.62's html reporter drops the title
|       option and buries config metadata in a base64 payload, so SS11's label would
|       otherwise be unverifiable in the file.
|     - scripts/run-matrix.ts: runs the 9 cells one at a time into separate report
|       folders, stamps each, writes reports/run-manifest.md, exits non-zero if any
|       cell failed.
|     - scripts/verify-reports.ts: independent SS11 gate over all nine reports.

* commit 65f4cf19d7a084d65a583186373a4448940ae0f6
| Author: Bảo Ngọc Hà <hiembaonon@gmail.com>
| Date:   Sun Jul 26 21:56:19 2026 +0700
| 
|     fix(usability): force the search step, don't just imply it
|     
|     Previous wording ("find 1 product you like, under X price") let
|     participants complete the whole task by clicking straight from the
|     5-product grid, never touching the search box — the "search by
|     keyword" step of the flow was never actually required to happen.
|     
|     Rewrote each task to assign a specific keyword the participant must
|     type into the search box (verified live: each returns exactly 1
|     match). Keywords vary in form across participants (full name, partial
|     name, Vietnamese diacritics, uppercase) to exercise different search
|     behaviors while keeping per-participant task difficulty identical.
|

* commit 4462b448c7972f1279250249628ba94fe985af23
| Author: Bảo Ngọc Hà <hiembaonon@gmail.com>
| Date:   Sun Jul 26 21:52:09 2026 +0700
| 
|     fix(usability): correct price bands in task-scenarios to match real catalog
|     
|     All 7 price thresholds (300,000₫-1,000,000₫) were far below the SUT's
|     actual cheapest product (Keychron Q1, 4,000,000₫) — verified live at
|     localhost:5173. No product could ever match, so the "search by
|     keyword" step of the flow would always return 0 results and no
|     participant could complete the task.
|     
|     Rewrote thresholds against the real catalog (4tr/6tr/28tr/30tr/45tr),
|     each yielding a different valid product set (1 product up to all 5)
|     to keep the per-participant variation the design already called for.
